CMPXCHG16B_LOCK (M128) - Latency

Operands

  • Operand 1 (r/w): Memory
  • Operand 2 (r/w, suppressed): Register (RDX)
  • Operand 3 (r/w, suppressed): Register (RAX)
  • Operand 4 (r, suppressed): Register (RCX)
  • Operand 5 (r, suppressed): Register (RBX)
  • Operand 6 (w, suppressed): Flags (ZF: w)

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, base register): ≤51

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, index register): ≤20

Latency operand 1 → 1 (memory): 15

Latency operand 1 → 2 (address, base register): 18

Latency operand 1 → 2 (address, index register): 17

Latency operand 1 → 2 (memory): ≤31

Latency operand 1 → 3 (address, base register): 17

Latency operand 1 → 3 (address, index register): 16

Latency operand 1 → 3 (memory): ≤30

Latency operand 1 → 6 (address, base register): 16

Latency operand 1 → 6 (address, index register): 15

Latency operand 2 → 1: ≤16

Latency operand 2 → 2: 14

Latency operand 2 → 3: 13

Latency operand 2 → 6: 12

Latency operand 3 → 1: ≤16

Latency operand 3 → 2: 14

Latency operand 3 → 3: 13

Latency operand 3 → 6: 12

Latency operand 4 → 1: ≤13

Latency operand 4 → 2: 2

Latency operand 4 → 3: 2

Latency operand 4 → 6: 1

Latency operand 5 → 1: ≤13

Latency operand 5 → 2: 3

Latency operand 5 → 3: 2

Latency operand 5 → 6: 1

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, base register): ≤51
